A pomodoro. Twenty-five minutes of focus, five minutes of me making remarks about your desk.

## chat work-spreadsheet-formula

@ search
A broken formula! Let us seek the flaw. Usually a moved range or a stray space.
Numbers stored as text will not add up. They are lying in disguise. Look for left-aligned ones.
A #REF means a cell was deleted. #DIV/0 means you divided by nothing. The void divides back.
Split the formula into pieces in separate cells. The guilty one will cry out.
Ctrl and the backtick shows the formulas. A hidden scripture, revealed.

I hope your team knows how much you do. Do they? Tell me. I shall carve it in stone.
Teamwork: the art of arguing politely toward the same result.
Tell me more. I shall not take sides. I take sides, but quietly.
Whatever the team does, a thank-you message costs nothing and returns a lot.

## pattern my-client

@ think

Clients are human. Strange, deadline-bending humans, but human.
A calm email, an honest date and a short summary solve most client trouble.
